为什么不是所有东西都放进 LLM？#

确定性的工作由普通程序完成：

schema validation。 · timestamp alignment。 · threshold comparison。 · commit/build lookup。 · ACL。 · release policy。 · metric calculation。

ML/LLM 只用于：

semantic retrieval。 · multimodal interpretation。 · unstructured evidence synthesis。 · hypothesis ranking。 · summary generation。

这是可靠性和可解释性的基本分工。

92. FastAPI Service Contract#

python
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import Annotated, Literal
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from fastapi import FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, HttpUrl

app = FastAPI(title="Release Triage Intelligence API")

class ArtifactType(str, Enum):
    LOG = "log"
    METRIC = "metric"
    IMAGE = "image"
    VIDEO = "video"
    TRACE = "trace"
    TEST_REPORT = "test_report"

class ArtifactRef(BaseModel):
    artifact_id: UUID
    artifact_type: ArtifactType
    uri: str
    checksum_sha256: str
    schema_version: str
    start_time: datetime | None = None
    end_time: datetime | None = None

class CreateTriageCaseRequest(BaseModel):
    release_id: str
    run_id: str
    build_id: str
    baseline_build_id: str | None = None
    scenario_id: str
    failure_time: datetime
    failure_code: str
    artifacts: list[ArtifactRef]
    priority: Literal["critical", "high", "normal"] = "normal"

class CreateTriageCaseResponse(BaseModel):
    case_id: UUID
    state: str
    workflow_id: str
    status_url: str

@app.post(
    "/v1/triage/cases",
    response_model=CreateTriageCaseResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED,
)
async def create_triage_case(
    request: CreateTriageCaseRequest,
    idempotency_key: Annotated[str, Header(alias="Idempotency-Key")],
) -> CreateTriageCaseResponse:
    request_hash = stable_request_hash(request)

existing = await case_repository.find_idempotent(
        release_id=request.release_id,
        idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
    )

if existing:
        if existing.request_hash != request_hash:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="Idempotency key reused with different request",
            )
        return existing.to_response()

validate_artifact_ownership(request.artifacts, request.release_id)

case_id = uuid4()
    workflow_id = f"triage-case-{case_id}"

await case_repository.create_with_outbox(
        case_id=case_id,
        request=request,
        request_hash=request_hash,
        idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
        workflow_id=workflow_id,
    )

return CreateTriageCaseResponse(
        case_id=case_id,
        state="QUEUED",
        workflow_id=workflow_id,
        status_url=f"/v1/triage/cases/{case_id}",
    )

Contract Design Details#

API 返回 202 Accepted，因为 video 和 historical retrieval 是 long-running。 · Idempotency-Key 防止 CI timeout 后重复创建 case。 · artifact 使用 URI + checksum，而不是通过 API 上传巨大文件。 · baseline_build_id 支持 candidate 与 known-good build 比较。 · workflow ID deterministic，避免重复启动 Temporal workflow。

93. Pydantic Analysis Output#

LLM 不应该返回自由文本。使用 strict typed output：

python
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, model_validator

class EvidenceCitation(BaseModel):
    evidence_id: str
    evidence_type: Literal[
        "log",
        "metric",
        "image",
        "video",
        "test_assertion",
        "incident",
        "code_change",
    ]
    observation: str
    timestamp_ms: int | None = None

class RootCauseHypothesis(BaseModel):
    component: str
    cause: str
    supporting_evidence: list[EvidenceCitation] = Field(min_length=1)
    contradicting_evidence: list[EvidenceCitation] = []
    missing_evidence: list[str] = []
    raw_model_confidence: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=1.0)

class TriageAnalysis(BaseModel):
    observed_failure: str
    hypotheses: list[RootCauseHypothesis] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=5)
    novel_failure: bool
    requires_expert_review: bool

@model_validator(mode="after")
    def require_review_for_novel_failure(self):
        if self.novel_failure and not self.requires_expert_review:
            raise ValueError("Novel failures require expert review")
        return self

后处理还要验证：

citation 是否属于当前 evidence bundle。 · timestamp 是否在 test run 范围。 · component 是否存在于 ownership registry。 · model 是否引用了不存在的 commit。 · raw confidence 是否经过 calibration。

94. Durable Workflow Implementation#

Temporal-style Python pseudocode：

python
from datetime import timedelta

from temporalio import workflow

@workflow.defn
class TriageWorkflow:
    @workflow.run
    async def run(self, case_id: str) -> dict:
        case = await workflow.execute_activity(
            load_case,
            case_id,
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(seconds=30),
        )

bundle = await workflow.execute_activity(
            build_evidence_bundle,
            case,
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=10),
            retry_policy=TRANSIENT_RETRY,
        )

analyses = await workflow.gather(
            workflow.execute_activity(
                analyze_logs,
                bundle,
                start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=3),
            ),
            workflow.execute_activity(
                analyze_metrics,
                bundle,
                start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=5),
            ),
            workflow.execute_activity(
                analyze_visuals,
                bundle,
                start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=10),
            ),
            workflow.execute_activity(
                compare_candidate_to_baseline,
                bundle,
                start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=10),
            ),
        )

incidents = await workflow.execute_activity(
            retrieve_similar_incidents,
            args=[bundle, analyses],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=3),
        )

hypotheses = await workflow.execute_activity(
            generate_hypotheses,
            args=[bundle, analyses, incidents],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=3),
            retry_policy=LLM_RETRY,
        )

calibrated = await workflow.execute_activity(
            calibrate_and_validate,
            args=[bundle, hypotheses, incidents],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=1),
        )

result = await workflow.execute_activity(
            persist_analysis_and_publish_event,
            args=[case_id, calibrated],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=1),
        )

return result

为什么 Workflow Engine 很重要？#

普通 task queue 只负责投递；durable workflow 还保存：

当前执行到哪个 step。 · activity result。 · retry timer。 · cancellation。 · human approval wait。 · workflow history。

如果分析跑 30 分钟后 worker crash，应该从最近成功 activity 继续，而不是重跑全部 video processing。

95. Evidence Bundle Data Structure#

python
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LogEvent:
    timestamp: datetime
    component: str
    severity: str
    template_id: str
    message: str
    attributes: dict[str, Any]

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MetricSeries:
    name: str
    timestamps_ms: list[int]
    values: list[float]
    unit: str
    source: str

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VideoFrame:
    frame_id: str
    timestamp_ms: int
    object_uri: str
    perceptual_hash: str

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvidenceBundle:
    case_id: str
    run_id: str
    release_id: str
    build_id: str
    scenario_id: str
    trigger_time_ms: int
    logs: tuple[LogEvent, ...]
    metrics: tuple[MetricSeries, ...]
    frames: tuple[VideoFrame, ...]
    failed_assertions: tuple[str, ...]
    lineage: dict[str, str]

frozen=True 表示 analysis input 在 workflow 中 immutable。任何新的 preprocessing 都生成新 bundle version，而不是原地修改 evidence。

Part 24 — Python AI Implementation for Triage#

96. Robust Time-Series Anomaly Detection#

下面实现 rolling median + MAD，适合对 outlier 更稳健的 metric anomaly detection：

python
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import NDArray

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AnomalyPoint:
    index: int
    value: float
    baseline: float
    score: float

def robust_anomalies(
    values: NDArray[np.float64],
    window: int = 101,
    threshold: float = 4.5,
) -> list[AnomalyPoint]:
    if window < 5 or window % 2 == 0:
        raise ValueError("window must be odd and >= 5")
    if len(values) < window:
        return []

half = window // 2
    result: list[AnomalyPoint] = []

for index in range(half, len(values) - half):
        local = values[index - half : index + half + 1]
        median = float(np.median(local))
        mad = float(np.median(np.abs(local - median)))

if mad < 1e-12:
            continue

score = 0.6745 * (float(values[index]) - median) / mad

if abs(score) >= threshold:
            result.append(
                AnomalyPoint(
                    index=index,
                    value=float(values[index]),
                    baseline=median,
                    score=score,
                )
            )

return result

Production Improvements#

对不同 metric 使用不同 window 和 threshold。 · 对 rate、counter、gauge 使用不同 preprocessing。 · 处理 missing values 和 sensor dropout。 · 将 scenario 和 speed range 纳入 baseline。 · 同时检测 point anomaly 与 sustained change。 · 保存 detector version 和 parameters。

Unit Test#

python
import numpy as np

def test_robust_anomalies_detects_spike():
    values = np.ones(201, dtype=np.float64)
    values += np.linspace(0.0, 0.1, 201)
    values[100] = 9.0

anomalies = robust_anomalies(
        values,
        window=51,
        threshold=4.0,
    )

assert any(point.index == 100 for point in anomalies)

def test_robust_anomalies_ignores_short_input():
    values = np.array([1.0, 1.1, 0.9])
    assert robust_anomalies(values, window=5) == []

97. Change Detection Between Candidate and Baseline#

使用 bootstrap 估计 metric difference confidence interval：

python
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import NDArray

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BootstrapDifference:
    observed_difference: float
    lower_95: float
    upper_95: float
    regression: bool

def bootstrap_mean_difference(
    candidate: NDArray[np.float64],
    baseline: NDArray[np.float64],
    *,
    higher_is_worse: bool,
    practical_threshold: float,
    samples: int = 5_000,
    seed: int = 7,
) -> BootstrapDifference:
    if len(candidate) == 0 or len(baseline) == 0:
        raise ValueError("candidate and baseline must be non-empty")

rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    differences = np.empty(samples, dtype=np.float64)

for i in range(samples):
        candidate_sample = rng.choice(
            candidate,
            size=len(candidate),
            replace=True,
        )
        baseline_sample = rng.choice(
            baseline,
            size=len(baseline),
            replace=True,
        )
        differences[i] = (
            candidate_sample.mean() - baseline_sample.mean()
        )

observed = float(candidate.mean() - baseline.mean())
    lower, upper = np.quantile(differences, [0.025, 0.975])

if higher_is_worse:
        regression = lower > practical_threshold
    else:
        regression = upper < -practical_threshold

return BootstrapDifference(
        observed_difference=observed,
        lower_95=float(lower),
        upper_95=float(upper),
        regression=regression,
    )

为什么同时需要 statistical significance 和 practical threshold？

样本非常大时，极小 difference 也可能 statistically significant。 · Release decision 关心 difference 是否达到工程上有意义的程度。 · threshold 应由 metric risk 和 domain owner 定义。

98. Log Template Normalization#

简单 regex 可以先处理常见 variable：

python
import re

UUID_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"\b[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-"
    r"[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-"
    r"[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-"
    r"[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-"
    r"[0-9a-fA-F]{12}\b"
)
HEX_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\b0x[0-9a-fA-F]+\b")
FLOAT_PATTERN = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])-?\d+\.\d+(?![\w.])")
INTEGER_PATTERN = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.])-?\d+(?![\w.])")
PATH_PATTERN = re.compile(r"(?:/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+){2,}")

def normalize_log_message(message: str) -> str:
    normalized = UUID_PATTERN.sub("<UUID>", message)
    normalized = HEX_PATTERN.sub("<HEX>", normalized)
    normalized = PATH_PATTERN.sub("<PATH>", normalized)
    normalized = FLOAT_PATTERN.sub("<FLOAT>", normalized)
    normalized = INTEGER_PATTERN.sub("<INT>", normalized)
    return " ".join(normalized.split())

但 production log mining 还需要：

component-specific parser。 · JSON structured fields。 · units 保留。 · error code 保留。 · stack trace frame normalization。 · multiline aggregation。 · template evolution。

不应该把所有数字都删除。例如 error code E1042 或 camera ID 可能是诊断关键。

99. Failure Sequence Extraction#

python
from collections.abc import Iterable
from datetime import timedelta

def extract_failure_sequence(
    events: Iterable[LogEvent],
    trigger_time: datetime,
    before: timedelta = timedelta(seconds=10),
    after: timedelta = timedelta(seconds=2),
) -> list[str]:
    start = trigger_time - before
    end = trigger_time + after

selected = sorted(
        (
            event
            for event in events
            if start <= event.timestamp <= end
            and event.severity in {"WARN", "ERROR", "FATAL"}
        ),
        key=lambda event: event.timestamp,
    )

sequence: list[str] = []

for event in selected:
        token = f"{event.component}:{event.template_id}"

if not sequence or sequence[-1] != token:
            sequence.append(token)

return sequence

可以进一步生成 sequence embedding，或使用 edit distance 比较 incidents。

Levenshtein distance 适合短 event sequence；Transformer encoder 适合更复杂的上下文，但要有足够 labeled data。

100. Keyframe Extraction with OpenCV#

python
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np

def extract_changed_keyframes(
    video_path: Path,
    output_dir: Path,
    *,
    sample_every_frames: int = 15,
    change_threshold: float = 18.0,
) -> list[Path]:
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

capture = cv2.VideoCapture(str(video_path))
    fps = capture.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS) or 30.0

previous_gray: np.ndarray | None = None
    outputs: list[Path] = []
    frame_index = 0

while True:
        ok, frame = capture.read()
        if not ok:
            break

if frame_index % sample_every_frames != 0:
            frame_index += 1
            continue

gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        gray = cv2.resize(gray, (320, 180))

changed = previous_gray is None
        if previous_gray is not None:
            difference = cv2.absdiff(gray, previous_gray)
            changed = float(difference.mean()) >= change_threshold

if changed:
            timestamp_ms = int(1_000 * frame_index / fps)
            output = output_dir / f"frame_{timestamp_ms:010d}.jpg"
            cv2.imwrite(str(output), frame)
            outputs.append(output)

previous_gray = gray
        frame_index += 1

capture.release()
    return outputs

这只是 scene-change heuristic。Automated-driving triage 还可能需要强制保留：

failure timestamp 前后固定 frames。 · perception confidence change 时刻。 · emergency behavior 时刻。 · assertion failure 时刻。 · sensor degradation 时刻。

不能只按视觉变化采样；静态画面中也可能发生关键 planning/control failure。

101. Image and Video Embeddings#

PyTorch-style batch inference：

python
from collections.abc import Sequence

import torch
from PIL import Image

@torch.inference_mode()
def encode_frames(
    paths: Sequence[str],
    *,
    model,
    processor,
    device: torch.device,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    images = [
        Image.open(path).convert("RGB")
        for path in paths
    ]

inputs = processor(
        images=images,
        return_tensors="pt",
        padding=True,
    )
    inputs = {
        key: value.to(device, non_blocking=True)
        for key, value in inputs.items()
    }

embeddings = model.get_image_features(**inputs)
    embeddings = torch.nn.functional.normalize(
        embeddings,
        p=2,
        dim=-1,
    )
    return embeddings.cpu()

Production considerations：

batch by resolution。 · decode 与 GPU inference pipeline parallel。 · model version 写入 embedding metadata。 · input checksum 去重。 · inference timeout。 · OOM split batch。 · corrupted frame quarantine。 · embedding index atomic version switch。

102. Multimodal Retrieval and Reciprocal Rank Fusion#

python
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchHit:
    incident_id: str
    score: float
    rank: int
    source: str

def reciprocal_rank_fusion(
    rankings: list[list[SearchHit]],
    *,
    rank_constant: int = 60,
) -> list[tuple[str, float]]:
    scores: dict[str, float] = defaultdict(float)

for ranking in rankings:
        for hit in ranking:
            scores[hit.incident_id] += (
                1.0 / (rank_constant + hit.rank)
            )

return sorted(
        scores.items(),
        key=lambda item: item[1],
        reverse=True,
    )

为什么用 RRF？

Elasticsearch BM25、Milvus cosine、metric shape distance 的 score scale 不同。 · RRF 只依赖 rank，避免直接比较不可校准的 raw scores。 · 实现简单、robust，适合作为第一版 fusion baseline。

后续可训练 learning-to-rank model，features 包括：

log lexical score。 · log embedding similarity。 · visual similarity。 · metric sequence distance。 · same scenario。 · same component version。 · incident resolution quality。 · temporal proximity。

103. Reranker Feature Model#

python
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingClassifier

FEATURE_NAMES = [
    "log_similarity",
    "metric_similarity",
    "visual_similarity",
    "same_scenario",
    "same_component_version",
    "same_failure_code",
    "resolution_verified",
]

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IncidentPair:
    features: list[float]
    relevant: int

def train_incident_reranker(
    examples: list[IncidentPair],
) -> HistGradientBoostingClassifier:
    x = np.asarray(
        [example.features for example in examples],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )
    y = np.asarray(
        [example.relevant for example in examples],
        dtype=np.int64,
    )

model = HistGradientBoostingClassifier(
        max_depth=6,
        learning_rate=0.05,
        max_iter=200,
        l2_regularization=1.0,
        random_state=7,
    )
    model.fit(x, y)
    return model

训练 labels 可以来自：

triager 点击并采用的 historical incident。 · linked duplicate incident。 · same final root cause。 · manually judged relevance。

注意 selection bias：只显示过的 incidents 才可能被点击。需要保留 exposure log，并使用人工 judgment set 验证。

104. Confidence Calibration in Python#

使用 logistic calibration：

python
import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import NDArray
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression

class ConfidenceCalibrator:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._model = LogisticRegression(
            C=1.0,
            class_weight="balanced",
            random_state=7,
        )

def fit(
        self,
        raw_features: NDArray[np.float64],
        correct: NDArray[np.int64],
    ) -> None:
        self._model.fit(raw_features, correct)

def predict(
        self,
        raw_features: NDArray[np.float64],
    ) -> NDArray[np.float64]:
        return self._model.predict_proba(raw_features)[:, 1]

Features：

text
LLM self-score
top retrieval similarity
top1-top2 margin
evidence count
modality coverage
analyzer agreement
known signature indicator
OOD score

LLM self-score 只能作为一个 feature，不能直接当最终 confidence。

Calibration Split#

不能在训练 reranker 的同一数据上评估 calibration：

text
train set
 -> train model

calibration set
 -> fit calibrator

held-out test set
 -> measure accuracy, ECE, Brier

105. Expected Calibration Error#

python
import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import NDArray

def expected_calibration_error(
    probabilities: NDArray[np.float64],
    labels: NDArray[np.int64],
    bins: int = 10,
) -> float:
    boundaries = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, bins + 1)
    total = len(probabilities)
    ece = 0.0

for lower, upper in zip(boundaries[:-1], boundaries[1:]):
        if upper == 1.0:
            mask = (probabilities >= lower) & (probabilities <= upper)
        else:
            mask = (probabilities >= lower) & (probabilities < upper)

count = int(mask.sum())
        if count == 0:
            continue

average_confidence = float(probabilities[mask].mean())
        empirical_accuracy = float(labels[mask].mean())

ece += (
            count / total
            * abs(average_confidence - empirical_accuracy)
        )

return ece

同时报告 reliability diagram，避免单一 ECE 隐藏局部问题。

106. Out-of-Distribution Detection#

新 failure 不应被强行归到最相似 old category。

简单 embedding distance baseline：

python
import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import NDArray

def ood_score(
    query: NDArray[np.float64],
    known_centroids: NDArray[np.float64],
) -> float:
    query = query / np.linalg.norm(query)
    centroids = known_centroids / np.linalg.norm(
        known_centroids,
        axis=1,
        keepdims=True,
    )

maximum_similarity = float((centroids @ query).max())
    return 1.0 - maximum_similarity

更完整的方法：

Mahalanobis distance。 · energy score。 · deep ensemble disagreement。 · conformal prediction。 · retrieval distance + metadata novelty。

OOD threshold 必须在 historical novel incidents 上评估，而不是凭直觉选择。

107. Evidence-Grounded LLM Prompt#

python
SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
You assist qualified release and triage engineers.

Rules:
1. Use only the supplied evidence.
2. Distinguish observations from hypotheses.
3. Every hypothesis must cite evidence IDs.
4. Include contradictory evidence.
5. Never claim a commit is causal without direct evidence.
6. If evidence is insufficient, say so.
7. Return the required JSON schema only.
"""

def build_triage_prompt(
    case,
    deterministic_analysis,
    similar_incidents,
) -> str:
    return render_json(
        {
            "case": {
                "build_id": case.build_id,
                "baseline_build_id": case.baseline_build_id,
                "scenario_id": case.scenario_id,
                "failure_code": case.failure_code,
            },
            "timeline": deterministic_analysis.timeline,
            "metric_anomalies": deterministic_analysis.metric_anomalies,
            "log_signatures": deterministic_analysis.log_signatures,
            "visual_observations": deterministic_analysis.visual_observations,
            "failed_assertions": deterministic_analysis.failed_assertions,
            "similar_resolved_incidents": similar_incidents,
        }
    )

还要对 output 做：

JSON schema validation。 · citation existence validation。 · forbidden unsupported field validation。 · length limit。 · sensitive information redaction。 · retry with repair prompt，仅限格式错误。

语义错误不应该通过“再问一次”自动视为修复。

108. Evidence Citation Validator#

python
class UnsupportedCitationError(ValueError):
    pass

def validate_citations(
    analysis: TriageAnalysis,
    allowed_evidence_ids: set[str],
) -> None:
    for hypothesis in analysis.hypotheses:
        citations = (
            hypothesis.supporting_evidence
            + hypothesis.contradicting_evidence
        )

for citation in citations:
            if citation.evidence_id not in allowed_evidence_ids:
                raise UnsupportedCitationError(
                    f"Unknown evidence: {citation.evidence_id}"
                )

更严格时，对每个 claim 做 Natural Language Inference / entailment check，但它仍然是 probabilistic guard，不代替人工审查。

109. Release Risk Aggregation#

python
from dataclasses import dataclass

SEVERITY_WEIGHT = {
    "critical": 10.0,
    "high": 5.0,
    "medium": 2.0,
    "low": 0.5,
}

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FailureCluster:
    severity: str
    estimated_probability: float
    scenario_exposure: float
    novelty: float
    unresolved: bool

def release_risk(clusters: list[FailureCluster]) -> float:
    total = 0.0

for cluster in clusters:
        unresolved_multiplier = 1.5 if cluster.unresolved else 1.0
        total += (
            SEVERITY_WEIGHT[cluster.severity]
            * cluster.estimated_probability
            * cluster.scenario_exposure
            * (1.0 + cluster.novelty)
            * unresolved_multiplier
        )

return total

这个 score 适合排序和 dashboard，不应独自决定安全 release。Hard safety rules 必须保持显式 policy。

111. Project B：Release Qualification Intelligence#

Goal#

比较 candidate build 与 baseline，生成可审计 readiness report。

Pipeline#

text
Release Candidate Created
 -> Select Required and Risk-Based Scenarios
 -> Run Simulations
 -> Aggregate Results
 -> Cluster Duplicate Failures
 -> Compare Against Baseline
 -> Detect New or Worsened Regressions
 -> Generate Qualification Report
 -> Apply Policy
 -> Human Review

Duplicate Failure Clustering#

同一个 underlying bug 可能导致数千 simulation failures。不能让 release dashboard 显示 3,000 个独立 incidents。

Feature：

failure code。 · log sequence embedding。 · anomalous metric names。 · scenario family。 · stack trace。 · visual condition。 · component version。

可从 hierarchical clustering 或 DBSCAN baseline 开始：

python
from sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN
from sklearn.preprocessing import normalize

def cluster_failures(embeddings: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    normalized = normalize(embeddings)

model = DBSCAN(
        eps=0.18,
        min_samples=2,
        metric="cosine",
        n_jobs=-1,
    )
    return model.fit_predict(normalized)

DBSCAN 优势：

不需要预先知道 cluster 数。 · 可以把 novel points 标成 noise。

问题：

eps 对 density 很敏感。 · 不同 failure families density 不同。 · 大规模 pairwise distance 成本高。

生产中可先 ANN candidate generation，再在局部 graph clustering。

Release Report#

json
{
  "release_id": "rc-2026-06-20.4",
  "candidate_build": "build-18421",
  "baseline_build": "build-18398",
  "test_coverage": {
    "required": 18240,
    "completed": 18192,
    "valid": 18120
  },

    "critical": 0,
    "high": 2,

}

112. Project C：Simulation Scenario Prioritization#

每次 code change 不可能运行所有 simulation scenarios。需要选择最有价值的 tests。

Inputs#

files/components changed。 · dependency graph。 · historical failure mapping。 · scenario coverage。 · recent flaky tests。 · severity。 · runtime cost。 · novelty。

Optimization#

在 compute budget 下最大化 risk coverage：

maximize \sum_i x_i Value_i

subject to：

\sum_i x_i Cost_i \le Budget
x_i \in \{0,1\}

这是 knapsack-style formulation。

Greedy baseline：

python
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScenarioCandidate:
    scenario_id: str
    expected_value: float
    compute_minutes: float
    mandatory: bool = False

def select_scenarios(
    candidates: list[ScenarioCandidate],
    budget_minutes: float,
) -> list[ScenarioCandidate]:
    selected = [
        scenario for scenario in candidates
        if scenario.mandatory
    ]
    used = sum(item.compute_minutes for item in selected)

optional = sorted(
        (
            scenario
            for scenario in candidates
            if not scenario.mandatory
        ),
        key=lambda item: (
            item.expected_value / max(item.compute_minutes, 1e-9)
        ),
        reverse=True,
    )

for scenario in optional:
        if used + scenario.compute_minutes <= budget_minutes:
            selected.append(scenario)
            used += scenario.compute_minutes

return selected

Safety-critical mandatory suite 永远不能被 learned prioritizer 删除。ML 只优化额外测试预算。

113. Project D：Regression Bisect Assistant#

Goal#

当 candidate build 出现 regression，缩小 suspected commit range。

Inputs#

last known good build。 · first known bad build。 · commits between builds。 · component ownership。 · changed files。 · test scenario。 · failure evidence。

Workflow#

text
Known Good ---------------- Known Bad
               |
         choose midpoint build
               |
        run targeted simulation
               |
       pass -> move lower bound
       fail -> move upper bound

Binary search 理想运行次数：

Runs \approx \lceil \log_2 N \rceil

若 128 commits，理想只需要约 7 次。

现实问题：

flaky test。 · interacting commits。 · build failure。 · non-monotonic behavior。 · expensive simulation。

因此每个 midpoint 可能需要重复运行，并使用 probability 判断，而不是一次 pass/fail。

Bayesian View#

每个 commit 有 prior probability；根据 changed component 与 failure evidence 更新 posterior。

LLM 可以总结 commit intent，但不能凭 commit message 宣布 causality。

114. Project E：Triage Feedback Learning Loop#

Triager 操作成为高价值 labels：

text
hypothesis accepted
hypothesis rejected
component changed
incident linked
missing evidence requested
case marked novel
final root cause
resolution commit

Event：

json
{
  "event_type": "triage_hypothesis_reviewed",
  "case_id": "case-481",
  "analysis_version": "analysis-v19",
  "hypothesis_rank": 1,

  "decision": "rejected",
  "correct_component": "localization",

}

使用方式：

evaluation dataset。 · calibrator update。 · reranker training。 · failure taxonomy improvement。 · missing evidence analysis。

不能自动把所有 human clicks 当 ground truth：

reviewer 可能修改后又撤回。 · 不同 reviewers 可能 disagree。 · final resolution 比初步 assignment 更可靠。 · label 需要 provenance 和 confidence。

Part 26 — Testing、Observability 与 Security#

115. Testing Pyramid#

Unit Tests#

log normalization。 · timestamp alignment。 · anomaly calculation。 · RRF。 · policy。 · confidence threshold。

Contract Tests#

Kafka event schema。 · FastAPI request/response。 · model output schema。 · object metadata。

Integration Tests#

PostgreSQL transaction + Outbox。 · Kafka duplicate delivery。 · Elasticsearch / Milvus retrieval。 · Temporal retry。 · object storage signed URL。

End-to-End Tests#

使用小型 synthetic case：

text
camera confidence drop
 -> perception warning
 -> planner fallback
 -> test assertion failure

验证：

evidence alignment。 · top hypothesis。 · citations。 · confidence。 · dashboard case。

Historical Replay#

使用 past resolved incidents，隐藏 final root cause，运行整条 pipeline，再比较结果。

116. Property-Based Tests#

适合 invariant：

python
from hypothesis import given
from hypothesis import strategies as st

@given(
    st.lists(
        st.floats(
            min_value=-1_000,
            max_value=1_000,
            allow_nan=False,
            allow_infinity=False,
        ),
        min_size=5,
        max_size=500,
    )
)
def test_robust_anomaly_scores_are_finite(values):
    array = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64)
    window = min(len(array), 11)
    if window % 2 == 0:
        window -= 1

anomalies = robust_anomalies(array, window=window)

assert all(np.isfinite(item.score) for item in anomalies)

其他 invariants：

citation 必须属于 case。 · release coverage 不超过 1。 · terminal state 不可回到 running。 · same idempotency request 只创建一个 case。 · tenant / project boundary 不可跨越。

117. Failure Injection#

必须测试：

  • Kafka message duplicated。
  • Kafka unavailable。
  • PostgreSQL commit timeout。
  • object artifact missing。
  • corrupted video。
  • GPU OOM。
  • vLLM timeout。
  • vector index unavailable。
  • stale model。
  • worker killed after external side effect。

示例期待：

text
Milvus unavailable
 -> lexical search continues
 -> analysis coverage records missing vector retrieval
 -> confidence decreases
 -> case remains reviewable

系统的目标不是“所有 dependency 永远正常”，而是 failure 时保持正确状态、清楚暴露缺失能力。

不要将 raw log、video path 中的敏感信息或高 cardinality artifact ID 全部作为 metric labels。

case backlog oldest age。 · required analysis coverage 降低。 · unsupported claim rate 上升。 · confidence distribution sudden shift。 · embedding/index freshness lag。 · Kafka consumer lag。 · workflow activity retry storm。

119. Data Security#

Automated-driving data 可能包含：

location。 · faces / license plates。 · internal software behavior。 · proprietary maps。 · unreleased build details。

Controls：

  • service identity + mTLS。
  • least-privilege artifact access。
  • encrypted storage。
  • signed URL short TTL。
  • audit log。
  • retention policy。
  • PII redaction。
  • environment separation。
  • no production artifact in developer laptop。
  • model-provider data contract。

LLM Data Boundary#

在调用 model 前：

python
def prepare_llm_evidence(bundle):
    assert bundle.data_classification <= INTERNAL_ALLOWED

redacted_logs = redact_sensitive_fields(bundle.logs)
    selected_metrics = aggregate_metrics(bundle.metrics)
    frame_descriptions = approved_visual_service.describe(
        bundle.frames
    )

return {
        "logs": redacted_logs,
        "metrics": selected_metrics,
        "visual_observations": frame_descriptions,
    }

是否允许 raw frame 进入 LLM/VLM 应由明确 data policy 决定，不能由 application developer 临时选择。

Part 27 — wolf Deep Interview Questions#

120. 为什么不能直接让 LLM 读取所有 Logs？#

回答：

context window 和 cost。 · logs 存在大量重复和 noise。 · timestamp/order 容易丢失。 · raw secrets / PII 风险。 · output 难以 reproduce。 · LLM 不擅长精确计算大量 time-series。

先使用 deterministic parser 和 statistical analyzer 压缩为 structured evidence，再让 LLM synthesis。

121. 如何区分 Root Cause 与 Symptom？#

使用：

temporal precedence。 · component dependency graph。 · counterfactual evidence。 · known failure propagation pattern。 · candidate/baseline change。 · intervention / bisect result。

仅仅“最先出现”也不一定是 root cause，因为某些 upstream signals 可能没有 log。

面试表达：

I would call the output a ranked root-cause hypothesis until it is supported by stronger causal evidence such as reproduction, code bisect, component isolation, or a verified fix.

122. 如何建立 Component Dependency Graph？#

Nodes：

sensor。 · perception。 · localization。 · prediction。 · planning。 · control。 · map。 · runtime/infrastructure。

Edges 表示 data/control dependency。

用途：

检查 hypothesis propagation 是否合理。 · ownership routing。 · 缩小 related logs。 · 识别 downstream symptom。

Graph 不能静态写死：

software architecture version。 · feature flag。 · vehicle configuration。 · fallback mode。

都可能改变有效 dependency。

123. SHAP 是否能解释整个 Triage System？#

不能。

SHAP 适合解释特定 tabular model prediction 的 feature contribution，但：

不解释 retrieval 数据为何存在。 · 不证明 causality。 · 不解释 LLM reasoning 的真实性。 · correlated features 会导致 attribution 难解释。

Triage system 更重要的解释形式：

source evidence。 · timeline。 · related resolved incidents。 · model/version lineage。 · contradictory evidence。 · confidence calibration。

124. 如何处理 Label Scarcity？#

historical resolved incidents。 · weak labels from issue links。 · heuristic labeling functions。 · expert annotation。 · active learning。 · self-supervised embeddings。 · retrieval-first system。

Active learning 选择：

high uncertainty。 · high severity。 · novel cluster。 · analyzer disagreement。 · high operational frequency。

不要只标最不确定样本，否则 dataset 可能偏离真实高价值 workload。

125. 如何设计 Human-in-the-Loop？#

Human review 不是最后加一个 approval button。

系统应优化：

reviewer 看到什么 evidence。 · 如何 correction。 · 如何记录 uncertainty。 · disagreement 如何处理。 · feedback 如何进入 model evaluation。

Case state：

text
ANALYZED
 -> NEEDS_REVIEW
 -> ASSIGNED
 -> INVESTIGATING
 -> ROOT_CAUSE_CONFIRMED
 -> FIX_LINKED
 -> VERIFIED
 -> CLOSED

AI 可以更新 suggestion，不能覆盖 human-confirmed root cause。

126. 如何衡量工具是否真的减少 Triage 时间？#

设计 controlled rollout：

baseline team 使用旧 workflow。 · treatment team 使用 AI-assisted workflow。 · 按 incident severity 和 category stratify。 · 比较 MTTU、MTTA、MTTR。 · 检查 escaped regression 和 false conclusion。

避免只比较使用工具前后，因为：

release complexity 可能不同。 · team composition 变化。 · incident severity 分布变化。

127. 如何处理 Flaky Simulation Tests？#

保存每次 run 的完整 config 和 seed。 · 重复运行估计 failure probability。 · 区分 deterministic regression 与 probabilistic failure。 · 对 flake 建独立 ownership。 · 不把 retry-until-pass 当解决方案。

Beta posterior：

先验：

p \sim Beta(\alpha,\beta)

观察 f 次 failure、s 次 success：

p \mid data \sim Beta(\alpha+f,\beta+s)

这样比 failures / runs 在小样本时更稳定。

128. 如何定位 Release 后才出现的 Regression？#

比较 dimensions：

build。 · region。 · hardware。 · vehicle configuration。 · scenario。 · time。 · traffic density。 · weather/light。

使用 progressive slicing 找到 effect concentration：

text
all failures
 -> only new build
 -> only camera hardware B
 -> only night scenario
 -> only exposure-control version v4

但要控制 multiple comparisons，避免在大量 slices 中偶然找到“显著”结果。

129. Kafka 在这个系统中提供什么，不提供什么？#

提供：

durable event log。 · replay。 · partition ordering。 · consumer decoupling。 · backpressure visibility via lag。

不提供：

end-to-end exactly-once external side effects。 · business transaction。 · unlimited capacity。 · artifact blob storage。 · automatic schema correctness。

大 video 放 Object Storage，Kafka event 只携带 URI、checksum 和 metadata。

130. Spark 与 Flink 在 wolf 场景如何分工？#

Flink：

live simulation completion。 · event-time aggregation。 · release progress。 · near-real-time anomaly。 · alert。

Spark：

candidate vs baseline historical comparison。 · large backfill。 · embedding regeneration。 · historical replay。 · offline evaluation dataset。

共享：

canonical schema。 · artifact lineage。 · data quality rules。

131. 如何保证 Dashboard 不误导？#

count 显示 denominator。 · candidate 与 baseline test mix 对齐。 · confidence interval。 · missing data visible。 · stale data timestamp。 · cluster count 与 raw event count 分开。 · AI hypothesis 明确标识。 · drill-down 到 source evidence。

2x failure increase 如果是从 1/10,000 到 2/10,000，与从 10% 到 20% 意义完全不同。

132. 如果只能先做一个 MVP，做什么？#

不先做全自动 LLM root-cause system。

第一版：

  1. canonical case model。
  2. evidence timeline。
  3. log template + metric comparison。
  4. similar resolved incident retrieval。
  5. engineer dashboard。
  6. feedback capture。

原因：

立即减少人工查找时间。 · 建立可评价的数据和 labels。 · 风险较低。 · 为后续 hypothesis generation 建立 grounded context。

133. Senior-Level Closing Answer#

My approach would be to build the evidence and evaluation foundation before promising autonomous root-cause analysis. I would first make every test failure reproducible and searchable through a canonical case model, synchronized multimodal evidence, build and scenario lineage, deterministic log and metric analysis, and retrieval of resolved incidents. Once that foundation is reliable, I would add an LLM as a bounded synthesis and hypothesis-ranking layer, with typed output, evidence citations, calibrated confidence, abstention, and human review. That produces useful value early while creating the feedback data needed to improve the AI safely.

Part 28 — AD/ADAS Domain Knowledge for a Platform Engineer#

134. Automated-Driving Software Stack#

面试不要求你假装是 controls 或 robotics specialist，但必须理解 failure evidence 从哪里产生。

text
Sensors
  camera | lidar | radar | GNSS | IMU | wheel odometry
        |
        v
Sensor Drivers and Synchronization
        |
        v
Perception
  detection | segmentation | tracking | lane estimation
        |
        v
Localization and Mapping
  vehicle pose | map matching
        |
        v
Prediction
  estimate future behavior of agents
        |
        v
Planning
  route | behavior | trajectory
        |
        v
Control
  steering | acceleration | braking
        |
        v
Vehicle Actuation

横跨所有 components：

health monitoring。 · safety supervisor。 · fallback / minimal-risk behavior。 · logging 和 telemetry。 · configuration / feature flags。 · map 和 model versions。

Triage 时必须区分：

upstream cause。 · downstream propagation。 · safety mechanism 正常触发。 · infrastructure/test harness failure。

135. Perception Failure Examples#

常见 evidence：

detection confidence drop。 · false positive / false negative。 · track ID switch。 · lane-boundary loss。 · depth / velocity error。 · sensor occlusion。 · glare、rain、night、motion blur。

基础 metrics：

Precision = TP / (TP + FP)
Recall = TP / (TP + FN)

Object detection 常使用 Intersection over Union：

IoU = \frac{Area(Prediction \cap GroundTruth)} {Area(Prediction \cup GroundTruth)}

但 release triage 不能只看 aggregate mAP。某个 rare safety-critical scenario 可能在 overall metric 中被平均掉。

136. Localization Failure Examples#

Localization 估计 vehicle pose：

text
position: x, y, z
orientation: roll, pitch, yaw
velocity
uncertainty / covariance

failure evidence：

pose jump。 · map-matching mismatch。 · GNSS degradation。 · IMU drift。 · covariance suddenly grows。 · timestamp mismatch。

若 localization 错误，perception objects 可能被投影到错误 map position，planner 随后产生异常轨迹。Triage 不能误判 planner 为第一原因。

137. Prediction and Planning Failures#

Prediction：

other agent trajectory inaccurate。 · uncertainty underestimated。 · multimodal futures collapse。 · pedestrian intent missed。

Planning：

no valid trajectory。 · collision constraint violated。 · oscillation。 · excessive conservatism。 · route divergence。 · latency deadline missed。

规划不是单纯“找最短路径”，通常是约束优化：

\min_{\tau} w_1 Cost_{safety}(\tau) + w_2 Cost_{comfort}(\tau) + w_3 Cost_{progress}(\tau) + w_4 Cost_{rules}(\tau)

subject to：

vehicle dynamics collision constraints road boundaries traffic rules

Triage platform 不需要求解 planning，但要展示哪个 cost/constraint 导致 trajectory rejection。

138. Control Failure Examples#

Control 将 planned trajectory 转为 steering、throttle、brake commands。

常见 metrics：

lateral error。 · longitudinal error。 · heading error。 · jerk。 · control command saturation。 · tracking delay。

Control failure 也可能是 upstream planned trajectory 不连续造成。需要比较：

commanded trajectory。 · actual vehicle state。 · actuator response。 · controller output。

139. Safety-Critical Testing Vocabulary#

SIL#

Software-in-the-Loop：软件在 simulated environment 中运行，快、规模大，适合 regression。

HIL#

Hardware-in-the-Loop：加入真实或近真实 hardware interface，验证 timing、driver、integration。

Closed-Loop Simulation#

system action 会改变后续 simulated world，适合评估 planning/control interaction。

Open-Loop Replay#

重放 recorded sensor data；system output 不改变后续输入。适合 perception regression，但不完整反映闭环行为。

Scenario#

对道路、actors、环境、初始状态和 expected behavior 的结构化定义。

ODD#

Operational Design Domain：系统被设计允许工作的道路、天气、速度、地区和环境范围。

Disengagement / Fallback#

系统退出正常 automated operation 或进入 minimal-risk behavior。它可能表示 upstream failure，也可能表示 safety mechanism 正常工作。

140. Determinism and Reproducibility#

Simulation failure 的 replay 需要保存：

  • software build。
  • container/image digest。
  • model versions。
  • map version。
  • scenario definition。
  • random seed。
  • simulator version。
  • hardware/resource profile。
  • feature flags。
  • external service versions。

相同 seed 仍不一定 bitwise deterministic，因为：

GPU non-deterministic kernels。 · concurrency ordering。 · floating-point reduction。 · network timing。

目标是说明 reproducibility level：

exact。 · behaviorally equivalent。 · statistically reproducible。

Part 29 — Release Engineering and Mainline Stability#

141. Mainline Stability 是什么？#

Mainline 是团队持续集成的主开发分支。稳定并不代表永远没有 bug，而是：

changes 快速集成。 · regression 快速发现。 · blocking failure 有清晰 owner。 · broken build 不长时间影响整个团队。 · release candidate 可从可解释状态产生。

Release & Triage Tooling 的价值是缩短：

text
change merged
 -> signal appears
 -> regression detected
 -> failure understood
 -> owner assigned
 -> fix verified

142. Release Qualification 和普通 CI Test 的区别#

普通 CI：

unit test。 · static analysis。 · small integration test。 · 快速 feedback。

Release qualification：

larger scenario coverage。 · cross-component integration。 · candidate vs baseline。 · simulation at scale。 · performance and safety metrics。 · unresolved incident assessment。 · explicit readiness decision。

不能把所有昂贵 simulation 放在每个 pull request 的 blocking path。应分层：

text
PR
 -> fast impacted tests

Merge
 -> broader integration and simulation

Release Candidate
 -> required qualification suite

Nightly / Continuous
 -> long-tail and exploratory scenarios

143. Test Impact Analysis#

根据 change 选择相关 tests：

text
changed files
 -> owning components
 -> dependency graph
 -> affected capabilities
 -> scenario mapping
 -> required tests

必须保留 mandatory safety suite；learned test selection 只优化额外 coverage。

错误风险：

dependency graph stale。 · hidden runtime coupling。 · config-only change 未映射。 · shared library impact 被低估。

因此应保留 random exploration 和 broad periodic suite。

144. Release Gate Fail-Open 还是 Fail-Closed？#

必须按 failure 类型区分：

known critical safety test fails：fail-closed。 · analysis service temporary unavailable：通常 needs manual review，不是自动 pass。 · optional dashboard delayed：不一定 block。 · required evidence missing：不能宣称 qualification complete。

面试表达：

I would not encode one global fail-open or fail-closed behavior. Each gate condition needs an explicit risk policy, and analysis-system failure must remain distinguishable from product-test failure.

145. Rollback and Fix Verification#

发现 regression 后：

  1. stop or quarantine affected rollout。
  2. identify last known good。
  3. revert / disable feature / change config。
  4. rerun targeted reproducer。
  5. rerun affected qualification slice。
  6. verify no new regression。
  7. update incident resolution。

Fix merged 不等于 failure resolved。必须有 reproduction scenario 上的 verified result。

Part 30 — Coding and Debugging Interview Problems#

146. Coding Problem：Merge Time Windows#

场景：多个 analyzers 返回异常时间窗口，需要合并重叠或相邻 intervals。

python
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True, order=True)
class TimeWindow:
    start_ms: int
    end_ms: int

def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.end_ms < self.start_ms:
            raise ValueError("end must not precede start")

def merge_windows(
    windows: list[TimeWindow],
    adjacency_ms: int = 0,
) -> list[TimeWindow]:
    if not windows:
        return []

ordered = sorted(windows)
    merged = [ordered[0]]

for current in ordered[1:]:
        previous = merged[-1]

if current.start_ms <= previous.end_ms + adjacency_ms:
            merged[-1] = TimeWindow(
                start_ms=previous.start_ms,
                end_ms=max(previous.end_ms, current.end_ms),
            )
        else:
            merged.append(current)

return merged

Complexity：

O(n \log n)

排序后 scan 是 O(n)。

追问：

streaming intervals 怎么处理？ · intervals 超大不能放内存怎么办？ · 不同 sensor clocks 如何先对齐？

147. Coding Problem：Top-K Failure Signatures#

python
from collections import Counter
import heapq

def top_k_failures(
    signatures: list[str],
    k: int,
) -> list[tuple[str, int]]:
    if k <= 0:
        return []

counts = Counter(signatures)
    return heapq.nlargest(
        k,
        counts.items(),
        key=lambda item: (item[1], item[0]),
    )

大规模 streaming 可使用：

partitioned aggregation。 · count-min sketch。 · approximate heavy hitters。 · Flink keyed state。

148. Coding Problem：Dependency Graph Root Candidates#

给定 error components 和 dependency edges，找没有其他 failed upstream parent 的 candidates。

python
from collections import defaultdict

def root_failure_candidates(
    failed_components: set[str],
    edges: list[tuple[str, str]],
) -> set[str]:
    upstream: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)

for source, target in edges:
        upstream[target].add(source)

roots = set()

for component in failed_components:
        failed_parents = (
            upstream[component] & failed_components
        )
        if not failed_parents:
            roots.add(component)

return roots

这只是 graph heuristic，不是 causal proof。缺少 telemetry 的 upstream node 可能不会出现在 failed set。

149. Coding Problem：Thread-Safe TTL Cache#

面试可先讲 contract，再写简化实现：

python
import threading
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Generic, TypeVar

K = TypeVar("K")
V = TypeVar("V")

@dataclass
class CacheEntry(Generic[V]):
    value: V
    expires_at: float

class TTLCache(Generic[K, V]):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._values: dict[K, CacheEntry[V]] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

def get(self, key: K) -> V | None:
        now = time.monotonic()

with self._lock:
            entry = self._values.get(key)
            if entry is None:
                return None

if entry.expires_at <= now:
                del self._values[key]
                return None

return entry.value

def put(self, key: K, value: V, ttl_seconds: float) -> None:
        if ttl_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError("ttl_seconds must be positive")

with self._lock:
            self._values[key] = CacheEntry(
                value=value,
                expires_at=time.monotonic() + ttl_seconds,
            )

追问：

memory bound / LRU。 · background cleanup。 · cache stampede。 · distributed cache。 · stale data。 · lock contention。

150. Debugging Problem：Consumer Lag 持续上升#

回答顺序：

  1. arrival rate 与 processing rate。
  2. partition 数和 active consumers。
  3. rebalance / crash。
  4. poison message。
  5. downstream latency。
  6. batch size / commit strategy。
  7. CPU、memory、network。
  8. recent deploy。

不要立刻说“加 consumer”。如果 partitions 已满或 downstream database 是瓶颈，加 consumer 无效。

151. Debugging Problem：模型输出质量突然下降#

检查：

  • model version。
  • preprocessing version。
  • input distribution。
  • index freshness。
  • prompt version。
  • dependency fallback。
  • quantization/runtime change。
  • corrupted artifact。
  • traffic mix。

先找 change point，再按 dimension slice。不要直接重新训练模型。

Part 31 — Explainability, Documentation, and Adoption#

152. Explainability 在 Triage 中意味着什么？#

不是展示 hidden chain-of-thought，而是提供可验证依据：

synchronized source evidence。 · observation/hypothesis separation。 · supporting and contradicting facts。 · similar resolved incidents。 · confidence calibration。 · analysis lineage。 · model limitations。

用户真正需要回答：

为什么这个 case 被归给我？ · 为什么系统怀疑这个 component？ · 它看了哪些数据？ · 哪些数据缺失？ · 我如何纠正？

153. Internal Tool Documentation#

岗位明确要求写 documentation 和 usage guidelines。一个可采用的内部工具至少需要：

Quick Start#

如何创建 case。 · 支持哪些 artifacts。 · status 在哪里看。 · 如何提供 feedback。

API Contract#

schema。 · authentication。 · idempotency。 · errors。 · rate limits。

Interpretation Guide#

confidence 的含义。 · hypothesis 不是 confirmed root cause。 · missing evidence。 · stale index warning。 · abstention。

Operational Runbook#

backlog。 · dependency outage。 · model rollback。 · index rebuild。 · replay。

Model Card#

intended use。 · excluded use。 · training/evaluation data。 · metrics by slice。 · known limitations。 · owner。 · version history。

154. Example Usage Guideline#

text
Use the tool to:
  organize evidence
  find related resolved incidents
  compare candidate and baseline
  prioritize investigation

Do not use the tool to:
  treat an unreviewed hypothesis as confirmed root cause
  bypass required safety review
  infer facts absent from evidence
  expose restricted artifacts to unauthorized users

155. Driving Adoption#

内部工具 adoption 失败常见原因：

workflow 之外的额外页面。 · result 不可信。 · 没有 source evidence。 · feedback 很麻烦。 · latency 太长。 · false positives 太多。

改进：

嵌入已有 issue tracker / CI 页面。 · deep link 到 exact timestamp。 · one-click accept/correct。 · 从 deterministic retrieval 开始建立 trust。 · 与 triagers 一起定义 taxonomy。 · 发布 usage metrics 和 limitation。

Part 32 — Behavioral and Cross-Functional Interview Answers#

156. “Tell me about a complex platform you owned.”#

使用 Amsterdam multimodal discovery story：

At Amsterdam, I worked on an extensible discovery platform spanning image, audio, video, 3D, and document assets across multiple products. The challenge was not only scale - more than 50 billion assets - but also creating one lifecycle across modality-specific ingestion, embeddings, lexical and vector indexes, retrieval, reranking, and serving. I helped design the distributed pipeline with Kafka, Flink, Milvus, Elasticsearch, Kubernetes, and tiered storage. I also worked on cross-modal retrieval and hybrid edge-cloud execution that reduced heavy-media payload transfer by 10-20x. The key lesson was to standardize contracts and lineage while allowing modality-specific processing. That pattern maps directly to multimodal test evidence at wolf.

如果被追问你的个人贡献，必须明确区分：

你设计了什么。 · 你亲自实现了什么。 · team 共同交付什么。 · 指标如何测量。

157. “Tell me about a difficult production issue.”#

可使用 apps observability / latency 经历，但只讲你能确认的事实：

text
Situation
  high-throughput data services had p95 latency and operational pressure

Task
  improve performance and production diagnosis

Action
  memory-efficient caching
  algorithm and resource-aware pipeline changes
  metrics, alerts, dashboards

Result
  p95 latency reduced 54%
  lower load on core services
  faster incident detection and resolution

面试前应补上你真实记得的：

exact bottleneck。 · metric used。 · rollout safety。 · one trade-off。

不要现场编造具体 incident timeline。

158. “Tell me about working with different teams.”#

回答应展示 interface thinking：

In shared AI infrastructure, model researchers, product teams, and platform engineers optimize for different things. Researchers care about model quality and experimentation speed; product teams care about user behavior and deadlines; platform teams care about reliability, capacity, and cost. I try to turn those needs into a measurable contract: supported inputs, quality target, latency budget, resource profile, failure behavior, evaluation data, and ownership. That lets the teams disagree on evidence and trade-offs rather than on vague expectations.

连接 wolf：

release engineers 定义 qualification process。 · triagers 定义 useful evidence。 · domain teams 定义 component semantics。 · infrastructure team 定义 pipeline reliability。

159. “What if a triager disagrees with the model?”#

I would treat the disagreement as valuable product and evaluation data, not as user error. The UI should let the triager identify the correct component, reject unsupported evidence, request missing signals, and link the final resolution. We should then distinguish retrieval failure, evidence-extraction failure, reasoning failure, and taxonomy disagreement. Only verified outcomes should become high-confidence labels.

160. “How do you handle ambiguity?”#

I first define the user decision the tool needs to support. For triage, that might be deciding where to investigate next, not automatically proving root cause. I then build a narrow vertical slice with explicit success metrics and failure boundaries. For example, I would begin with evidence alignment and similar-incident retrieval, measure Recall@k and time-to-understand, collect corrections, and only then add generated hypotheses.

161. “How do you balance speed and quality?”#

I separate reversible from irreversible decisions. A dashboard prototype or shadow analysis can move quickly. A release-blocking policy, cross-tenant access rule, or safety-critical conclusion needs stronger tests, lineage, review, and rollback. I also use progressive rollout so speed does not require exposing the entire organization to an unvalidated change.

162. “Tell me about using AI coding tools responsibly.”#

I use AI coding tools as accelerators for bounded tasks such as test scaffolding, repetitive adapters, schema conversion, documentation drafts, and refactoring alternatives. I do not treat generated code as trusted. I review it against the intended contract, run unit and integration tests, check dependency and license implications, benchmark critical paths, and apply extra scrutiny to concurrency, authorization, data deletion, and release-gate logic. The engineer remains accountable for the result.

163. Questions to Ask the Interviewer#

这些问题也能展示你理解岗位：

  1. What currently consumes the most triager time: evidence collection, duplicate detection, ownership assignment, or root-cause investigation?
  2. How are simulation and on-road incidents represented today?
  3. What decisions are advisory versus release-blocking?
  4. How is final root cause captured and linked back to the original test?
  5. Which modalities are hardest to analyze together?
  6. How do you measure the quality of current triage?
  7. What is the expected boundary between deterministic rules, ML models, and LLM analysis?
  8. How does the team handle novel failures and low-confidence output?
  9. Which CI/CD and simulation systems would this tooling integrate with?
  10. What would successful impact look like in the first six months?

165. What You Must Be Able to Explain Without Notes#

这不是时间计划，而是 readiness test。你应能直接解释：

  1. 一条 simulation failure 如何从 Kafka event 变成 triage case。
  2. 为什么先构建 evidence bundle，再调用 LLM。
  3. 如何对齐 log、metric 和 video timestamps。
  4. 如何检索 similar resolved incidents。
  5. confidence 为什么需要 calibration。
  6. 什么情况下系统必须 abstain。
  7. candidate 与 baseline 如何公平比较。
  8. 如何处理 flaky tests。
  9. release gate 如何区分 product failure 和 analysis failure。
  10. 你的 Amsterdam 50B+ multimodal platform 如何迁移到此问题。
  11. 你的 Amazon/Microsoft experience 如何证明 scale 和 diagnostics 能力。
  12. 你没有 automotive experience 时如何诚实回答。

下面这套准备，你可以把它当成这个岗位的 canonical system design answer。核心题目我建议你按这个来练：

Design an LLM-driven Release & Triage Platform for AD/ADAS release qualification and failure root-cause analysis.

设计一个内部平台，把 CI/CD、simulation testing、on-road testing 里的 logs / metrics / traces / test results / images / video 变成可解释的 failure summary、root-cause hypotheses、regression detection、release risk signal，帮助 release engineers / triagers / developers 决定是否可以 release。

wolf 的公开信息里，Arene 被定位为统一 automotive software development platform，覆盖 build、test、validate、deploy、improve 的 continuous loop；Cloud & AI 又强调 governed environment 下的数据准备、训练、验证和模型部署；这和这个岗位的 release & triage tooling 非常贴。([Wolf][1]) 另外 wolf 的 AD 页面强调 safety-governed physical AI，AI decision-making system 外面有 safety framework 和 validated constraints，这一点在面试里可以借来强调：LLM 负责分析和建议，release gate 不能完全交给 LLM。 ([Wolf][2])

1. 这个岗位真正想听什么#

这个 role 表面是 LLM + internal tooling，但本质是：

把复杂自动驾驶 release signals 变成 engineering decision support system。

你要展示四种能力：

  1. Infra / platform sense

能接 CI/CD、simulation platform、data pipeline、dashboard、issue tracker、artifact store。

  1. AI/ML / LLM practicality

不只是“call OpenAI API”，而是懂 RAG、multimodal processing、confidence scoring、eval、feedback loop、prompt/versioning、model fallback。

  1. Release engineering / safety mindset

LLM 不能直接 block/unblock release。最终 gate 要靠 deterministic rules + statistical signal + human approval。

  1. Debugging / RCA thinking

能从 logs、metrics、video、image、test result、recent code changes、historical incidents 里做 evidence-based hypotheses。

你可以把你的背景自然贴进去：Cruise/GM 的 fleet tooling、vehicle health monitoring、deployment/progress tracking、CI/CD、BuildKite/Harness、calibration/Health Tunnel、real-time monitoring，这些都非常接近这个岗位。

2. 推荐的高阶面试开场#

可以这样说：

我会把这个系统设计成一个 AI-assisted release & triage platform，不是一个 autonomous release decision maker。

系统会从 CI/CD、simulation、on-road events、logs、metrics、images/video、code changes、historical incidents 里收集 signals，做 normalization、correlation、retrieval、classification、root-cause hypothesis generation 和 confidence scoring。

最终输出给 release engineers 和 triagers 的是：failure summary、evidence、suspected owner、similar past incidents、regression likelihood、release risk score，以及 recommended next actions。

对于 safety-critical workflow，LLM 的输出必须可追踪、可解释、可评估，并且重要 release gate 要保留 deterministic policy 和 human-in-the-loop。

这段非常关键，能把你从“会做 LLM app”的候选人，提升到“懂 safety-critical internal AI tooling”的候选人。

3. Requirements#

Functional requirements#

系统需要支持：

  1. Ingest signals

CI/CD build result · unit/integration/e2e test result · large-scale simulation test result · on-road event / incident · logs、metrics、traces · images、video clips、sensor metadata · code diffs、commit history、owners、release branch metadata

  1. Normalize failure events

把不同来源的 failure 统一成 FailureEvent · 关联 build、commit、scenario、vehicle/software version、test suite、artifact links

  1. RCA assistant

summarize failure · identify suspicious components · retrieve similar historical failures · compare against baseline / previous release · generate root-cause hypotheses with evidence

  1. Regression detection

判断是不是 new regression · 判断是否和 recent commit / config change / data drift / simulation environment change 有关

  1. Release qualification

aggregate release health · produce readiness score / risk score · show blocker trends · support release gate decision

  1. Dashboard & workflow

triage queue · owner assignment · confidence signal · link to Jira / ticket / PR / logs / video · human feedback: correct / incorrect / duplicate / known issue

  1. Evaluation & governance

track LLM accuracy · hallucination detection · audit log · prompt/model versioning · PII / sensitive data redaction

Non-functional requirements#

  1. Reliability

CI/CD and release workflow cannot depend on flaky LLM call · LLM failure should degrade gracefully

  1. Scalability

large-scale simulation can generate millions of test cases and artifacts · video/image processing must be async

  1. Latency

CI failure summary: minutes · release dashboard aggregation: near real-time · deep video analysis: async, possibly tens of minutes

  1. Traceability
  • every conclusion must link back to logs, metrics, video timestamp, test case, commit, or historical incident
  1. Security

internal code/logs may be sensitive · strict ACL, tenant/team isolation, audit trail

  1. Cost control

do not send raw huge logs/video to LLM · pre-process, summarize, retrieve only relevant chunks · cache repeated analysis

4. Back-of-the-envelope estimation#

面试时不要跳过 BOTE。可以给一个合理假设：

假设每天：

CI builds: 2,000 / day · Test runs: 200,000 / day · Simulation scenarios: 5M / day · Failure events: 50,000 / day · On-road notable events: 1,000 / day · Logs per failed run: 10 MB compressed · Video clips per interesting event: 30 sec to 2 min · Image/keyframes per event: 10–100 frames

Storage:

Raw logs: 50,000 × 10 MB = 500 GB/day · Simulation metadata: 5M × 5 KB = 25 GB/day · Video/keyframes: depends heavily, but raw video should stay in object storage, not DB · Embeddings: 50,000 events × 20 chunks × 1536 dim × 4 bytes ≈ 6 GB/day before index overhead

Conclusion:

Raw artifacts go to Object Storage / Data Lake · Metadata goes to OLTP + OLAP · Searchable text chunks go to Vector DB / Search index · Hot status and dashboard summaries can use Redis / cache · Heavy LLM/multimodal processing must be async queue-based
